| Avatar of Elon MuskAvatar of Elon Musk | **Elon Musk** (@elonmusk) tweeted: |
| --- | --- |

> My commitment to free speech extends even to not banning the account following my plane, even though that is a direct personal safety risk

November 6, 2022[^cite_note-plane-254]&#x20;

Musk is an outlier among business leaders who typically avoid partisan political advocacy.[^cite_note-255][^cite_note-256][^cite_note-257] Rejecting the [conservative](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conservatism_in_the_United_States) label, Musk has described himself as a [political moderate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_moderate),[^cite_note-258] even as his views have become more [right-wing](56522.md) over time.[^cite_note-Right-wing-259] His views have been characterized as [libertarian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Libertarianism_in_the_United_States) and [far-right](221220.md),[^cite_note-Libertarian-260][^cite_note-Far-right-261] and after [his involvement in European politics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_activities_of_Elon_Musk), they have received criticism from world leaders such as [Emmanuel Macron](43671127.md) and [Olaf Scholz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olaf_Scholz).[^cite_note-olaf-262][^cite_note-Henley-2025-263][^cite_note-264][^cite_note-265]

Within the context of [American politics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Views_of_Elon_Musk#U.S._politics), Musk supported Democratic candidates up until 2022, at which point he voted for a Republican for the first time.[^cite_note-mc-266][^cite_note-267] He has stated support for [universal basic income](28310828.md),[^cite_note-uni2-268] [gun rights](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gun_rights),[^cite_note-269] [freedom of speech](21401843.md),[^cite_note-270] a [tax on carbon emissions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tax_on_carbon_emissions),[^cite_note-271] and [H-1B visas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/H-1B_visa).[^cite_note-CBS_News-2024-272] Musk has expressed concern about issues such as [artificial intelligence](1164.md) (AI)[^cite_note-wp2015-273] and [climate change](5042951.md),[^cite_note-274] and has been a critic of [wealth tax](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wealth_tax),[^cite_note-275] [short-selling](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Short-selling),[^cite_note-276] and [government subsidies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_subsidies).[^cite_note-277] An immigrant himself, Musk has been accused of being [anti-immigration](6491626.md), and regularly blames [immigration policies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Immigration_policy_of_the_United_States) for [illegal immigration](5044573.md).[^cite_note-Fearmongering-278] He is also a [pronatalist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pronatalist) who believes [population decline](720599.md) is the biggest threat to civilization,[^cite_note-Pronatlist-279] and identifies as a [cultural Christian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cultural_Christian).[^cite_note-O'Mahony2024-280][^cite_note-281] Musk has long been an advocate for [space colonization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Space_colonization), especially the [colonization of Mars](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Colonization_of_Mars). He has repeatedly pushed for humanity colonizing Mars, in order to become an interplanetary species and lower the risks of [human extinction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_extinction).[^cite_note-SpaceX-282]

Musk has [promoted conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Views_of_Elon_Musk#Conspiracy_theories) and [made controversial statements](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Views_of_Elon_Musk#Race_and_white_nationalism) that have led to accusations of [racism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Racism), [sexism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexism), [antisemitism](1078.md),[^cite_note-283][^cite_note-Truth-284] [transphobia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Transphobia),[^cite_note-285] disseminating [disinformation](142690.md), and support of [white pride](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_pride).[^cite_note-Proud-286][^cite_note-Derbyshire-2025-287] While describing himself as a "pro-Semite",[^cite_note-CNBC160523-288] his comments regarding [George Soros](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Soros) and [Jewish communities](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_communities) have been condemned by the [Anti-Defamation League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Defamation_League) and the [Biden White House](65732174.md).[^cite_note-Condemned-289] Musk was criticized during the COVID-19 pandemic for making unfounded epidemiological claims,[^cite_note-kolodny-2020a-290] defying [COVID-19 lockdowns](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/COVID-19_lockdowns) restrictions,[^cite_note-291] and supporting the [Canada convoy protest](69876497.md) against [vaccine mandates](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/COVID-19_vaccine_mandates).[^cite_note-292][^cite_note-293] He has amplified false claims of [white genocide](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_genocide_conspiracy_theory#South_Africa) in South Africa.[^cite_note-294]

Musk was a registered [independent voter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Independent_voter) when he lived in California.[^cite_note-NBCNewsMail-295] Historically, he has donated to both [Democrats](5043544.md) and [Republicans](32070.md),[^cite_note-huffingtonpost.com-296] many of whom serve in states in which he has a vested interest.[^cite_note-Peters-2022-297] Musk supported Democrat [Barack Obama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barack_Obama) in [2008](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2008_US_presidential_election) and [2012](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_United_States_presidential_election),[^cite_note-mc-266] Democrat [Hillary Clinton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hillary_Clinton) in [2016](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016_United_States_presidential_election), and Democrat [Joe Biden](145422.md) in [2020](48410011.md).[^cite_note-298] In the [2020 Democratic Party presidential primaries](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020_Democratic_Party_presidential_primaries), Musk endorsed candidate [Andrew Yang](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrew_Yang) and expressed support for Yang's proposed [universal basic income](28310828.md),[^cite_note-bloomberg.com-299] and endorsed [Kanye West](523032.md)'s [2020 presidential campaign](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kanye_West_2020_presidential_campaign).[^cite_note-300]

On August 5, 2021, Biden signed an [Executive Order](210541.md) targeting that half of cars sold in 2030 should be electric.[^cite_note-fe-301] [General Motors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/General_Motors) CEO [Mary Barra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mary_Barra) was in attendance.[^cite_note-fe-301] Musk commented, "seems odd that Tesla wasn't invited."[^cite_note-302] Barra said in 2024 Musk never forgave not being invited.[^cite_note-303][^cite_note-304] In 2025, former vice president [Kamala Harris](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kamala_Harris) said that she believed that not inviting Musk was a "big mistake."[^cite_note-Big_Mistake-305][^cite_note-306] In September 2021, Musk criticized Biden as "not the friendliest administration."[^cite_note-Big_Mistake-305] In December 2021, Musk publicly expressed opposition to the [Build Back Better Act](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Build_Back_Better_Act).[^cite_note-307][^cite_note-308]

Since 2022, his political contributions have mostly supported Republicans, with his first vote for a Republican going to [Mayra Flores](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mayra_Flores) in the [2022 Texas's 34th congressional district special election](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Texas's_34th_congressional_district_special_election).[^cite_note-309][^cite_note-310] In 2022, Musk gave over \$50 million to [Citizens for Sanity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Citizens_for_Sanity), a conservative [political action committee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_action_committee).[^cite_note-mattioli-311] In 2023, he supported Republican [Ron DeSantis](36729527.md) for the [2024 U.S. presidential election](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_US_presidential_election), giving \$10 million to his campaign,[^cite_note-mattioli-311] and hosted DeSantis's campaign announcement on a [Twitter Spaces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twitter_Spaces) event.[^cite_note-312][^cite_note-WaPoDesantis-313][^cite_note-314] From June 2023 to January 2024, Musk hosted a bipartisan set of X Spaces with Republican and Democratic candidates, including [Robert F. Kennedy Jr.](522298.md),[^cite_note-315] [Vivek Ramaswamy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vivek_Ramaswamy),[^cite_note-316] and [Dean Phillips](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dean_Phillips).[^cite_note-317]

In 2024, he started supporting international [far-right](221220.md) political parties, activists, and causes,[^cite_note-Far-right=2025-318] and has shared far-right misinformation[^cite_note-319][^cite_note-320][^cite_note-321] and numerous [conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy_theories).[^cite_note-322][^cite_note-323] Since 2024, [his views](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Views_of_Elon_Musk) have been generally described as right-wing.[^cite_note-324]

| Avatar of Elon MuskAvatar of Elon Musk | **Elon Musk** (@elonmusk) tweeted: |
| --- | --- |

> I fully endorse President Trump and hope for his rapid recovery

July 13, 2024[^cite_note-325]

By the middle of 2024, Musk had become a vocal and financial supporter of Donald Trump.[^cite_note-326] In July 2024, minutes after Trump survived [an assassination attempt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Attempted_assassination_of_Donald_Trump_in_Pennsylvania) while speaking in [Butler, Pennsylvania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Butler,_Pennsylvania), Musk [endorsed him for president](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Donald_Trump_2024_presidential_campaign_endorsements), saying: "I fully endorse President Trump and hope for his rapid recovery."[^cite_note-327][^cite_note-fucked-328] During the [presidential campaign](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump_2024_presidential_campaign), Musk joined Trump on stage at a campaign rally,[^cite_note-329] and during the campaign promoted conspiracy theories and falsehoods about Democrats, [election fraud](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Election_denial_movement_in_the_United_States)[^cite_note-Meyer_11012024-330] and immigration, in support of Trump.[^cite_note-331][^cite_note-332] Musk was the largest individual donor of the 2024 election, spending \$291 million.[^cite_note-Thadani_12062024-333][^cite_note-334] One PAC funded by Musk created and aired deceptive "[false-flag](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/False_flag)" advertisements appearing to come from progressive organizations in order to misrepresent Kamala Harris's positions.[^cite_note-335][^cite_note-336] Jacob Silverman, author of the 2025 book *Gilded Rage: Elon Musk and the Radicalization of Silicon Valley*, said that the tech industry represented by Musk, Thiel, [Andreessen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marc_Andreessen) and other capitalists, actually flourished under Biden, but the tech leaders chose Trump for their common ground on cultural issues.[^cite_note-337]

In 2023, Musk said he shunned the [World Economic Forum](172185.md) (WEF) because it was boring. The organization commented that they had not invited him since 2015.[^cite_note-338] Musk made a last-minute WEF debut in an interview with the organization's interim co-chair [Larry Fink](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Larry_Fink) during the [2026 WEF](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/56th_World_Economic_Forum).[^cite_note-339][^cite_note-340] He also participated in [Dialog](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dialog_\(organization\)), dubbed "Tech [Bilderberg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bilderberg_Meeting)" and organized by [Peter Thiel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Thiel) and [Auren Hoffman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Auren_Hoffman).[^cite_note-341][^cite_note-342]

Starting in January 2025, Musk's international political actions and comments came under increasing scrutiny and criticism, especially from the governments and leaders of France, Germany, Norway, Spain and the United Kingdom, particularly due to his position in the [U.S. government](78298108.md) from January to May 2025,[^cite_note-Musk-bids-farewell-343] as well as ownership of X.[^cite_note-Times-344][^cite_note-European-345][^cite_note-AfD-346] A February 2025 NBC News analysis found he had boosted far-right political movements to cut immigration and curtail regulation of business in at least 18 countries on six continents since 2023.[^cite_note-347]

In 2025, Musk contributed \$19 million to the [Wisconsin Supreme Court race](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Wisconsin_Supreme_Court_election), hoping to influence the state's future [redistricting](667093.md) efforts and its regulations governing car manufacturers and dealers.[^cite_note-348][^cite_note-349] Musk spent over \$90.6 million in the [2026 United States elections](70357296.md) in support of Republican candidates, donating primarily to [America PAC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/America_PAC), [Congressional Leadership Fund](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Congressional_Leadership_Fund), and [Senate Leadership Fund](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Senate_Leadership_Fund).[^cite_note-350]

### Salute at Trump's second inauguration

Main article: [Elon Musk salute controversy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elon_Musk_salute_controversy)



Musk giving a gesture at the [second inauguration of Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_inauguration_of_Donald_Trump) before saying "My heart goes out to you. It is thanks to you that the future of civilization is assured."

[^cite_note-cpdxzjw9p47o-351]

[^cite_note-nx-s1-5269719-352]

During his speech after the [second inauguration of Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_inauguration_of_Donald_Trump), Musk twice made a gesture interpreted by many as a [Nazi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nazi_salute) or a fascist [Roman salute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_salute).[^cite_note-354] He thumped his right hand over his heart, fingers spread wide, and then extended his right arm out, emphatically, at an upward angle, palm down and fingers together. He then repeated the gesture to the crowd behind him. As he finished the gestures, he said to the crowd, "My heart goes out to you. It is thanks to you that the future of civilization is assured."[^cite_note-cpdxzjw9p47o-351][^cite_note-nx-s1-5269719-352][^cite_note-355]

It was widely condemned as an intentional Nazi salute in Germany,[^cite_note-356][^cite_note-Bennhold-2025-357][^cite_note-358] where [making such gestures is illegal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Strafgesetzbuch_section_86a).[^cite_note-rbb24.de-359] The [Anti-Defamation League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Defamation_League) said it was not a Nazi salute,[^cite_note-ns-360] but other Jewish organizations disagreed and condemned the salute.[^cite_note-361][^cite_note-362][^cite_note-363][^cite_note-364] American public opinion was divided on partisan lines as to whether it was a fascist salute.[^cite_note-365] Musk dismissed the accusations of [Nazi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nazi) sympathies, deriding them as "dirty tricks" and a "tired" attack.[^cite_note-366][^cite_note-Fortinsky-2025a-367] [Neo-Nazi](54361.md) and [white supremacist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_supremacist) groups celebrated it as a Nazi salute.[^cite_note-ns-360][^cite_note-Condon-2025-368] Multiple European political parties demanded that Musk be banned from entering their countries.[^cite_note-369][^cite_note-370]

### Department of Government Efficiency

Main article: [Department of Government Efficiency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Department_of_Government_Efficiency)



Musk wielding a chainsaw at the [Conservative Political Action Conference](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conservative_Political_Action_Conference) (CPAC) in 2025, imitating a publicity stunt used by [Javier Milei](56599840.md) symbolic of efficiency, [federal mass layoffs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_United_States_federal_mass_layoffs) and [tax cutting](455990.md)

The concept of DOGE emerged in a discussion between Musk and [Donald Trump](4848272.md), and in August 2024, Trump committed to giving Musk an advisory role, with Musk accepting the offer.[^cite_note-Shepardson-371] In November and December 2024, Musk suggested that the organization could help to cut the [U.S. federal budget](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_federal_budget), consolidate the number of federal agencies,[^cite_note-372][^cite_note-373] and eliminate the [Consumer Financial Protection Bureau](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Consumer_Financial_Protection_Bureau),[^cite_note-374][^cite_note-375] and that its final stage would be "deleting itself".[^cite_note-376]

In January 2025, the organization was created by [executive order](210541.md), and Musk was designated a "[special government employee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special_government_employee)".[^cite_note-MD2-377][^cite_note-Ngo_1212025-378] Musk led the organization and was a [senior advisor to the president](29024072.md),[^cite_note-379] although [his official role](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_activities_of_Elon_Musk) is not clear.[^cite_note-Wolf2-380] In sworn statement during a lawsuit, the director of the White House [Office of Administration](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Office_of_Administration) stated that Musk "is not an employee of the U.S. DOGE Service or U.S. DOGE Service Temporary Organization", "is not the U.S. DOGE Service administrator", and has "no actual or formal authority to make government decisions himself".[^cite_note-381][^cite_note-382] Trump said two days later that he had put Musk in charge of DOGE.[^cite_note-383] A federal judge has ruled that Musk acted as the *de facto* leader of DOGE.[^cite_note-384]

Musk's role in the [second Trump administration](78298108.md), particularly in [response to DOGE](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Response_to_the_Department_of_Government_Efficiency), has [attracted public backlash](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Protests_against_Elon_Musk). He was criticized for his treatment of federal government employees,[^cite_note-nyt-young-aides-25-385][^cite_note-nyt-swan-musk-feb-25-386][^cite_note-wp-zs-25-387] including his influence over the [mass layoffs of the federal workforce](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_United_States_federal_mass_layoffs).[^cite_note-388][^cite_note-389][^cite_note-390] He has prioritized secrecy within the organization[^cite_note-The_New_York_Times-391] and has accused others of violating privacy laws.[^cite_note-MD2-377] A Senate report alleged that Musk could avoid up to \$2 billion in legal liability as a result of DOGE's actions.[^cite_note-392] In May 2025, [Bill Gates](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bill_Gates) accused Musk of "killing the world's poorest children" through his cuts to [USAID](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_Agency_for_International_Development).[^cite_note-393] Modeling by [Boston University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boston_University) estimated the cuts resulted in 400,000 children and 200,000 adults deaths by November.[^cite_note-394][^cite_note-395][^cite_note-usaid-shutdown-396] Musk departed on May 28, 2025, when the special government employee's 130 day deadline expired.[^cite_note-397][^cite_note-398][^cite_note-399]

### Feud with Donald Trump

Main article: [Trump–Musk feud](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trump%E2%80%93Musk_feud)

| Avatar of Elon MuskAvatar of Elon Musk | **Elon Musk** (@elonmusk) tweeted: |
| --- | --- |

> Time to drop the really big bomb:&#x20;
>
> @realDonaldTrump is in the Epstein files. That is the real reason they have not been made public.
>
> &#x20;Have a nice day, [DJT](4848272.md)!

June 5, 2025[^cite_note-400]

After leaving office, Musk criticized the Trump administration's [Big Beautiful Bill](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One_Big_Beautiful_Bill_Act), calling it a "disgusting abomination" due to its provisions increasing the deficit.[^cite_note-401] A feud began between Musk and Trump, with its most notable event being Musk alleging Trump had ties to sex offender [Jeffrey Epstein](6253522.md) on X (formerly Twitter) on June 5, 2025.[^cite_note-Epstein1-402][^cite_note-Epstein2-403] Trump responded on [Truth Social](69077351.md) stating that Musk went "CRAZY" after the "EV Mandate" was purportedly taken away and threatened to cut Musk's government contracts.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEPager2025d-404][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEPager2025e-405] Musk then called for a third Trump impeachment.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBasu2025-406] The next day, Trump stated that he did not wish to reconcile with Musk, and added that Musk would face "very serious consequences" if he funds Democratic candidates.[^cite_note-407] On June 11, Musk publicly apologized for the tweets against Trump before deleting them, saying they "went too far".[^cite_note-408]

### International relations

Main article: [International relations of Elon Musk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_relations_of_Elon_Musk)



Musk with Israeli president [Isaac Herzog](4645588.md) in November 2023

Musk has been critical of [Israel's actions](74998519.md) in the [Gaza Strip](12047.md) during the [Gaza war](74998519.md),[^cite_note-Robertson-20232-409] praised China's economic and climate goals,[^cite_note-He-20212-410][^cite_note-Choi-20192-411] suggested that [Taiwan](25734.md) and China should resolve [cross-strait relations](2596399.md),[^cite_note-Liang-20222-412][^cite_note-Khalaf-20222-413] and was described as having a close relationship with the [Chinese government](23239.md).[^cite_note-He-20212-410][^cite_note-Choi-20192-411]

In Europe, Musk expressed support for Ukraine in 2022 during the [Russian invasion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine), recommended referendums and peace deals on the annexed [Russia-occupied territories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian-occupied_territories_of_Ukraine),[^cite_note-Oshin-20222-414][^cite_note-Wilson-20222-415] and supported the far-right [Alternative for Germany](38708229.md) political party in 2024.[^cite_note-Gold-20242-416]

Regarding [British politics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/British_politics), Musk blamed the [2024 UK riots](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_United_Kingdom_riots) on [mass migration](1247786.md) and [open borders](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Open_border),[^cite_note-Gregory-20242-417][^cite_note-BBC_News-2024a2-418] criticized Prime Minister [Keir Starmer](18681491.md) for what he described as a "two-tier" policing system,[^cite_note-The_Independent-20242-419][^cite_note-The_Daily_Telegraph-20242-420][^cite_note-BBC_News-2024a2-418] and was subsequently attacked as being responsible for spreading [misinformation](2203174.md) and amplifying the far-right.[^cite_note-BBC_News2-421] Musk has publicly supported far-right activist [Tommy Robinson](32537879.md), and initially pledged financial backing to [Reform UK](59757202.md) ahead of the next general election. In January 2025, he publicly fell out with Reform UK leader [Nigel Farage](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nigel_Farage), calling for Farage to be replaced as party leader.[^cite_note-BBC_News-20252-422][^cite_note-Turvill-20242-423] Musk subsequently shifted his backing towards [Restore Britain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Restore_Britain), the political party launched by [Rupert Lowe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rupert_Lowe).[^cite_note-424]

In February 2026, Musk described Spanish Prime Minister [Pedro Sánchez](43297512.md) as a "tyrant" following his proposal to [prohibit minors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Social_media_age_verification_laws_by_country#Spain) under the age of 16 from accessing social media platforms.[^cite_note-425]

## Legal affairs

Main article: [Legal affairs of Elon Musk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Legal_affairs_of_Elon_Musk)

In July 2018, Musk arranged for SpaceX and the Boring Company employees to build a mini-submarine to assist in the rescue of [children trapped in a flooded cavern in Thailand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tham_Luang_cave_rescue). Despite him drawing attention to it on social media,[^cite_note-426] the mini-submarine was deemed not practical for the rescue mission.[^cite_note-ArsTechSubmarine-427] A British diver interviewed about the mission, Vern Unsworth, reflected on Musk's actions collectively a "PR stunt". In response, Musk referred to Unsworth as a "[pedo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pedophilia)" in a tweet.[^cite_note-428] In September, Unsworth filed a [defamation](28661.md) suit seeking \$190 million in damages.[^cite_note-429][^cite_note-430] Musk apologized and a jury later found Musk was not liable for the claim.[^cite_note-431][^cite_note-432]

In September 2018, Musk [was sued](5533631.md#Lawsuits_and_controversies) by the [U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U.S._Securities_and_Exchange_Commission) (SEC) for a [tweet](68533868.md) stating that funding had been secured for potentially taking Tesla private.[^cite_note-Goldstein_27-9-18-146][^cite_note-436] The securities fraud lawsuit characterized the tweet as false, misleading, and damaging to investors, and sought to bar Musk from being CEO of [publicly traded companies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_company).[^cite_note-Goldstein_27-9-18-146][^cite_note-437][^cite_note-OkaneLopatto18-438] Two days later, Musk settled with the SEC, without admitting or denying the SEC's allegations. As a result, Musk and Tesla were fined \$20 million each, and Musk was forced to step down for three years as Tesla chairman but was able to remain as CEO.[^cite_note-ct-147] Shareholders filed a lawsuit over the tweet,[^cite_note-439] and in February 2023, a jury found Musk and Tesla not liable.[^cite_note-440] Musk has stated in interviews that he does not regret posting the tweet that triggered the SEC investigation.[^cite_note-441][^cite_note-442]

In 2019, Musk stated in a tweet that Tesla would build half a million cars that year.[^cite_note-443] The SEC reacted by asking a court to hold him [in contempt](7201.md) for violating the terms of the 2018 settlement agreement. A joint agreement between Musk and the SEC eventually clarified the previous agreement details,[^cite_note-444] including a list of topics about which Musk needed preclearance.[^cite_note-445] In 2020, a judge blocked a lawsuit that claimed a tweet by Musk regarding Tesla stock price ("too high [imo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_acronyms:_I)") violated the agreement.[^cite_note-446][^cite_note-447] [Freedom of Information Act](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Freedom_of_Information_Act_\(United_States\)) (FOIA)-released records showed that the SEC concluded Musk had subsequently violated the agreement twice by tweeting regarding "[Tesla's solar roof](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tesla_Energy) production volumes and its stock price".[^cite_note-448]

In October 2023, the SEC sued Musk over his refusal to testify a third time in an investigation into whether he violated federal law by purchasing Twitter stock in 2022.[^cite_note-Brodkin-2023-449][^cite_note-450][^cite_note-Regulator_sues_Musk_to_force_testimony_in_X_probe-2023-451] In February 2024, Judge Laurel Beeler ruled that Musk must testify again.[^cite_note-452] In January 2025, the SEC filed a lawsuit against Musk for securities violations related to his purchase of Twitter.[^cite_note-453]

In January 2024, Delaware judge [Kathaleen McCormick](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kathaleen_McCormick) ruled in a 2018 lawsuit that Musk's \$55 billion pay package from Tesla be rescinded.[^cite_note-454] McCormick called the compensation granted by the company's board "an unfathomable sum" that was unfair to shareholders.[^cite_note-455] The [Delaware Supreme Court](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delaware_Supreme_Court) overturned McCormick's decision in December 2025, restoring Musk's compensation package and awarding \$1 in [nominal damages](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nominal_damages).[^cite_note-456] Musk exercised the stock options from his 2018 pay package on June 16, 2026.[^cite_note-457][^cite_note-458]

On March 20, 2026, a jury in [San Francisco, California](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/San_Francisco,_California), found Musk liable for misleading investors during his [acquisition of Twitter](70614210.md), though they absolved him of some fraud allegations, finding he did not "scheme" to defraud investors.[^cite_note-misleadverdict-459][^cite_note-lat-jury-finds-elon-musk-misled-investors-during-twitter-purchase-460] Specifically, Musk was found to have misled shareholders in two tweets, but not in a podcast, as the views he expressed in that were deemed by the jury to be an opinion.[^cite_note-misleadverdict-459][^cite_note-lat-jury-finds-elon-musk-misled-investors-during-twitter-purchase-460] Damages have yet to be calculated, but plaintiffs' lawyers estimated them to be \$2.5 billion.[^cite_note-461]

On May 18, 2026, a jury in [Oakland, California](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oakland,_California), decided in favor of OpenAI and its executives, finding that Musk's [February 2024 claims](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Musk_v._Altman) that the company violated its founding agreement by prioritizing profits over [AI safety](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AI_safety)[^cite_note-462] were barred by the [statute of limitations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Statute_of_limitations).[^cite_note-\:12-463]

## Personal life



Official [Royal Society](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Society) portrait, 2018

Musk became a U.S. citizen in 2002.[^cite_note-ii-54] From the early 2000s until late 2020, Musk resided in California, where both Tesla and SpaceX were founded.[^cite_note-bloomberg20201208-464] He then relocated to [Cameron County, Texas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cameron_County,_Texas),[^cite_note-465][^cite_note-466] saying that California had become "complacent" about its economic success.[^cite_note-bloomberg20201208-464][^cite_note-467][^cite_note-468]

While hosting *[Saturday Night Live](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saturday_Night_Live)* in 2021, Musk stated that he has [Asperger syndrome](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asperger_syndrome).[^cite_note-469] He was not diagnosed as a child.[^cite_note-470] When asked about his experience growing up with Asperger's syndrome the following year, Musk stated that "the social cues were not intuitive ... I would just tend to take things very literally ... but then that turned out to be wrong—\[people were not] simply saying exactly what they mean, there's all sorts of other things that are meant, and \[it] took me a while to figure that out."[^cite_note-471] Musk suffers from back pain and has undergone several spine-related surgeries, including a [disc](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Intervertebral_disc) replacement.[^cite_note-472][^cite_note-473] In 2000, he contracted a severe case of [malaria](20423.md) while on vacation in South Africa.[^cite_note-474]

Musk has stated he uses doctor-prescribed [ketamine](16948.md) for occasional depression and that he doses "a small amount once every other week or something like that";[^cite_note-475] since January 2024, some media outlets have reported that he takes ketamine, [marijuana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marijuana), [LSD](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LSD), [ecstasy](10024.md), [mushrooms](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hallucinogenic_mushrooms), [cocaine](7701.md) and other drugs. Musk at first refused to comment on his alleged drug use, before responding that he had not [tested](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drug_test) positive for drugs, and that if drugs somehow improved his productivity, "I would definitely take them!"[^cite_note-476] *[The New York Times](30680.md)*' investigations revealed Musk's overuse of ketamine and numerous other drugs, as well as strained family relationships and concerns from close associates who have become troubled by his public behavior as he became more involved in political activities and government work.[^cite_note-477] According to *The Washington Post*, President Trump described Musk as "a big-time drug addict".[^cite_note-478]

Through his own label Emo G Records, Musk released a [rap](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rap) track, "RIP [Harambe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harambe)", on [SoundCloud](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SoundCloud) in March 2019.[^cite_note-479][^cite_note-480][^cite_note-Fitzgerald-481] The following year, he released an [EDM](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Electronic_dance_music) track, "Don't Doubt Ur Vibe", featuring his own lyrics and vocals.[^cite_note-Etherington-482]

Musk plays video games, which he stated has a "'restoring effect' that helps his 'mental calibration'".[^cite_note-483] Some games he plays include [*Quake*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quake_\(series\)), *[Diablo IV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diablo_IV)*, *[Elden Ring](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elden_Ring)*, and [*Polytopia*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Battle_of_Polytopia).[^cite_note-484][^cite_note-485] Musk once claimed to be one of the world's top video game players but has since admitted to "[account boosting](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boosting_\(video_games\))", or cheating by hiring outside services to achieve top player rankings.[^cite_note-486][^cite_note-487][^cite_note-Gizmodo_Paying-488] Musk has justified the boosting by claiming that all top accounts do it so he has to as well to remain competitive.[^cite_note-489][^cite_note-Gizmodo_Paying-488][^cite_note-490] In 2024 and 2025, Musk criticized the video game *[Assassin's Creed Shadows](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassin's_Creed_Shadows)* and its creator [Ubisoft](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ubisoft) for "woke" content.[^cite_note-PC_Gamer_Ratio-491] Musk posted to X that "DEI kills art" and specified the inclusion of the historical figure [Yasuke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yasuke) in the *Assassin's Creed* game as offensive; he also called the game "terrible". Ubisoft responded by saying that Musk's comments were "just feeding hatred" and that they were focused on producing a game not pushing politics.[^cite_note-492][^cite_note-493]

### Marriages, relationships and children

Further information: [Musk family](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Musk_family)



Musk and his son X Æ A-Xii with President [Donald Trump](4848272.md) in the [Oval Office](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oval_Office) in February 2025

Musk has fathered at least 14 children, one of whom died as an infant.[^cite_note-494] *The Wall Street Journal* reported in 2025 that sources close to Musk suggest that the "true number of Musk's children is much higher than publicly known".[^cite_note-Mattioli_16APR2025-3] He had six children with his first wife, Canadian author [Justine Wilson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justine_Wilson), whom he met while attending Queen's University in Ontario, Canada; they married in 2000.[^cite_note-Streisand-2015-495] In 2002, their first child, Nevada Alexander Musk, died of [sudden infant death syndrome](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sudden_infant_death_syndrome) at the age of 10 weeks.[^cite_note-sids-496][^cite_note-holywoodlife2021-497] After his death, the couple used [in vitro fertilization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/In_vitro_fertilisation) (IVF) to continue their family;[^cite_note-holywoodlife2021-497] they had twins in 2004, followed by triplets in 2006.[^cite_note-holywoodlife2021-497] The couple divorced in 2008 and have [shared custody](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joint_custody_\(United_States\)) of their children.[^cite_note-498][^cite_note-499] The elder twin he had with Wilson [came out](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gender_identity) as a [trans woman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trans_woman) and, in 2022, officially [changed her name](58589.md) to [Vivian Jenna Wilson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vivian_Wilson),[^cite_note-06-23-22-500] adopting her mother's surname because she no longer wished to be associated with Musk.[^cite_note-06-23-22-500]

Musk began dating English actress [Talulah Riley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Talulah_Riley) in 2008.[^cite_note-501] They married two years later at [Dornoch Cathedral](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dornoch_Cathedral) in Scotland.[^cite_note-marriage-502][^cite_note-503] In 2012, the couple divorced, then remarried the following year.[^cite_note-talulah-504] After briefly filing for divorce in 2014,[^cite_note-talulah-504] Musk finalized a second divorce from Riley in 2016.[^cite_note-505] Musk then dated the American actress [Amber Heard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amber_Heard) for several months in 2017;[^cite_note-506] he had reportedly been "pursuing" her since 2012.[^cite_note-hollywood-507]

In 2018, Musk and Canadian musician [Grimes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grimes) confirmed they were dating.[^cite_note-508] Grimes and Musk have three children, born in 2020, 2021, and 2022.[^cite_note-513] Musk and Grimes originally gave their eldest child the name "X [Æ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%C3%86) A-12", which would have violated California regulations as it contained characters that are not in the modern English alphabet;[^cite_note-514][^cite_note-515] the names registered on the birth certificate are "X" as a first name, "Æ A-[Xii](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_numerals)" as a middle name, and "Musk" as a last name.[^cite_note-516][^cite_note-xaea-xii.cnet-517] They received criticism for choosing a name perceived to be impractical and difficult to pronounce;[^cite_note-518] Musk has said the intended pronunciation is "X Ash A Twelve".[^cite_note-xaea-xii.cnet-517] Their second child was born via [surrogacy](1514981.md).[^cite_note-GrimesVanityFair2022-519] Despite the pregnancy, Musk confirmed reports that the couple were "semi-separated" in September 2021; in an interview with [*Time*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_\(magazine\)) in December 2021, he said he was single.[^cite_note-520][^cite_note-521] In October 2023, Grimes sued Musk over parental rights and custody of X Æ A-Xii.[^cite_note-522][^cite_note-523][^cite_note-524] Musk has taken X Æ A-Xii to multiple official events in Washington, D.C. during [Trump's second term in office](78298108.md).[^cite_note-525]

Also in July 2022, *The Wall Street Journal* reported that Musk allegedly had an affair with [Nicole Shanahan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nicole_Shanahan), the wife of Google co-founder [Sergey Brin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sergey_Brin), in 2021, leading to their divorce the following year.[^cite_note-526] Musk denied the report.[^cite_note-527] Musk also had a relationship with Australian actress [Natasha Bassett](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Natasha_Bassett), who has been described as "an occasional girlfriend".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEIsaacson20237,_265-528] In October 2024, *The New York Times* reported Musk bought a Texas compound for his children and their mothers,[^cite_note-529] though Musk denied having done so.[^cite_note-530]

Musk also has four children with [Shivon Zilis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shivon_Zilis), director of operations and special projects at [Neuralink](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neuralink): twins born via [IVF](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/In_vitro_fertilisation) in 2021, a child born in 2024 via surrogacy and a child born in 2025.[^cite_note-537] In July 2026 Musk described Zilis as his "partner".[^cite_note-538]

On February 14, 2025, [Ashley St. Clair](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ashley_St._Clair), an influencer and author, posted on X claiming to have given birth to Musk's son Romulus five months earlier, which media outlets reckoned as Musk's thirteenth child.[^cite_note-542] On February 22, 2025, it was reported that St Clair had filed for sole custody of her five-month-old son and for Musk to be recognised as the child's father.[^cite_note-543][^cite_note-544] On March 31, 2025, Musk wrote that, while he was unsure if he was the father of St. Clair's child, he had paid St. Clair \$2.5 million and would continue paying her \$500,000 per year.[^cite_note-549] Later reporting from the *Wall Street Journal* indicated that \$1 million of these payments to St. Clair were structured as a loan.[^cite_note-Mattioli_16APR2025-3]

### Involvement with Jeffrey Epstein

See also: [List of people named in the Epstein files](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_people_named_in_the_Epstein_files)

In 2014, Musk and [Ghislaine Maxwell](32018562.md) appeared together in a photograph taken at an Academy Awards after-party, which Musk later described as a "[photobomb](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Photobombing)".[^cite_note-550][^cite_note-vj-551] The January 2026 [Epstein files](80415168.md) contain emails between Musk and Epstein from 2012 to 2013, after [Epstein's first conviction](6253522.md#First_criminal_case_\(2005%E2%80%932011\)).

Emails released on January 30, 2026, indicated that Epstein invited Musk to visit his private island on multiple occasions.[^cite_note-552] The correspondence showed that while Epstein repeatedly encouraged Musk to attend, Musk did not visit the island. In one instance, Musk discussed the possibility of attending a party with his then-wife [Talulah Riley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Talulah_Riley) and asked which day would be the "wildest party"; according to the emails, the visit did not take place after Epstein later cancelled the plans.[^cite_note-556]

On [Christmas day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christmas_day) in 2012, Musk emailed Epstein asking "Do you have any parties planned? I've been working to the edge of sanity this year and so, once my kids head home after Christmas, I really want to hit the party scene in [St Barts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/St._Barts) or elsewhere and let loose. The invitation is much appreciated, but a peaceful island experience is the opposite of what I'm looking for".[^cite_note-Robins-Early-554] Epstein replied that the "ratio on my island" might make Musk's wife uncomfortable to which Musk responded, "Ratio is not a problem for Talulah".[^cite_note-Robins-Early-554]

On September 11, 2013, Epstein sent an email asking Musk if he had any plans for coming to New York for the opening of the [United Nations General Assembly](31957.md), saying that many "interesting people" would be coming to Epstein's house. Musk responded that "Flying to NY to see UN diplomats do nothing would be an unwise use of time".[^cite_note-Rascius2026-557] Epstein responded by stating "Do you think i  \[*[sic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sic)*] am retarded. Just kidding, there is no one over 25 and all very cute."[^cite_note-Rascius2026-557]

Musk has denied any close relationship with Epstein and described him as a "creep" who attempted to ingratiate himself with influential people.[^cite_note-vj-551] When Musk was asked in 2019 if he introduced Epstein to [Mark Zuckerberg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mark_Zuckerberg), Musk responded: "I don't recall introducing Epstein to anyone, as I don't know the guy well enough to do so."[^cite_note-Rascius2026-557] The released emails nonetheless showed cordial exchanges on a range of topics, including Musk's inquiry about parties on the island.[^cite_note-Robins-Early-554][^cite_note-558][^cite_note-vj-551] The correspondence also indicated that Musk suggested hosting Epstein at SpaceX, while Epstein separately discussed plans to tour SpaceX and bring "the girls", though there is no evidence that such a visit occurred.[^cite_note-559] Musk has described the release of the files a "distraction", later accusing the [second Trump administration](78298108.md) of suppressing them to protect powerful individuals, including Trump himself.[^cite_note-563]

## Wealth

These paragraphs are an excerpt from [Wealth of Elon Musk](76211626.md).\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wealth_of_Elon_Musk\&action=edit)]

Elon Musk is the wealthiest person in the world in terms of [financial assets](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paper_wealth), with an estimated [net worth](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Net_worth) of US\$942 billion according to the *[Bloomberg Billionaires Index](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bloomberg_Billionaires_Index)*[^cite_note-564] and \$955 billion according to *[Forbes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes)*,[^cite_note-Wealth_of_Elon_Musk_ForbesWealth-565] as of September 21, 2026, primarily from his ownership stakes of 42% of [SpaceX](832774.md)[^cite_note-566] and 20% of [Tesla](5533631.md).[^cite_note-567] He will receive additional SpaceX stock[^cite_note-568] and \$1 trillion worth of Tesla stock[^cite_note-569] if the companies meet certain milestones.

Musk was first listed on the *[Forbes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes)* list of *[The World's Billionaires](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_World's_Billionaires)* in 2012, with a net worth of \$2 billion.[^cite_note-Wealth_of_Elon_Musk_Twice-570] According to *Forbes*, his net worth reached \$300 billion in 2021; \$400bn in December 2024;[^cite_note-Wealth_of_Elon_Musk_reach-571] \$500bn in October 2025;[^cite_note-572] \$600bn[^cite_note-573] and \$700bn[^cite_note-574] in December 2025; \$800bn in February 2026;[^cite_note-575] and \$900bn in June 2026, and he became the only [US dollar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/US_dollar) [trillionaire](47418795.md) in history that month upon the [initial public offering (IPO) of SpaceX](82660746.md).[^cite_note-576][^cite_note-Wealth_of_Elon_Musk_Trillionaire-577][^cite_note-578] Since then, his net worth has been volatile, largely because of changes in SpaceX’s valuation. His net worth reached an intraday peak of \$1.45 trillion in the week after the IPO,[^cite_note-579] but by late July 2026, a SpaceX stock collapse had reduced this net worth by \$750 billion, the largest net worth loss in history.[^cite_note-580][^cite_note-Wealth_of_Elon_Musk_\:3-581][^cite_note-582]

In May 2020, Musk pledged to sell almost all physical possessions and said he would "own no house",[^cite_note-Wealth_of_Elon_Musk_cash-583] but in February 2026, *[The New York Times](30680.md)* identified at least 37 companies that own properties which appear to be for Musk's personal use, including expensive [condominiums](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Condominium), planes used by him for [private travel](247003.md) and at least 1000 acres of land.[^cite_note-584] His wealth is managed by Excession LLC, his [family office](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Family_office), which has been run by [Jared Birchall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jared_Birchall) since 2016.[^cite_note-585]

### Musk Foundation

Main article: [Musk Foundation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Musk_Foundation)

The Musk Foundation is a United States–based charitable [foundation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foundation_\(nonprofit\)) funded and directed primarily by Musk. The foundation is dedicated to promoting [renewable energy](25784.md), [crewed space exploration](28431.md), [pediatrics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pediatrics), science and engineering education, and the "development of safe [artificial intelligence](1164.md) for the benefit of humanity".[^cite_note-secretive_foundation-586]

At the end of 2024, the Musk Foundation had over \$14 billion in assets. Since 2020, donations have largely been sent to organizations affiliated with Musk and his businesses.[^cite_note-587]

## Public image

Main article: [Public image of Elon Musk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_image_of_Elon_Musk)



Musk with President [Donald Trump](4848272.md) in May 2025

Although his ventures have been highly influential within their separate industries starting in the 2000s, Musk only became a public figure in the early 2010s. He has been described as an eccentric who makes spontaneous and impactful decisions, while also often making controversial statements, contrary to other billionaires who prefer reclusiveness to protect their businesses. Musk's actions and [his expressed views](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Views_of_Elon_Musk) have made him a polarizing figure.[^cite_note-Trouble-588] Biographer [Ashlee Vance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ashlee_Vance) described people's opinions of Musk as polarized due to his "part philosopher, part [troll](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Troll_\(slang\))" persona on Twitter.[^cite_note-celebrity-589] He has drawn denouncement for using his platform to mock the self-selection of [personal pronouns](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Preferred_gender_pronoun),[^cite_note-590] while also receiving praise for bringing international attention to matters like British survivors of [grooming gangs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grooming_gangs_scandal).[^cite_note-591]

Musk has been described as an American [oligarch](22315.md) due to his extensive influence over public discourse, social media, industry, politics, and government policy.[^cite_note-592] After Trump's re-election, [Musk's influence and actions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_activities_of_Elon_Musk#United_States) during the transition period and the [second presidency of Donald Trump](78298108.md) led some to call him "President Musk", the "actual president-elect", "shadow president" or "co-president".[^cite_note-593][^cite_note-594]

### Accolades

Main article: [List of awards and honors received by Elon Musk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_awards_and_honors_received_by_Elon_Musk)

Musk wearing a medal

Musk receiving the [Order of Defence Merit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_Defence_Merit) from the [Brazilian Armed Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brazilian_Armed_Forces) in 2022

[^cite_note-Costa2022-595]

Awards for his contributions to the development of the [Falcon rockets](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Falcon_rocket) include the [American Institute of Aeronautics and Astronautics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_Institute_of_Aeronautics_and_Astronautics) George Low Transportation Award in 2008,[^cite_note-596] the [Fédération Aéronautique Internationale](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/F%C3%A9d%C3%A9ration_A%C3%A9ronautique_Internationale) Gold Space Medal in 2010,[^cite_note-597] and the [Royal Aeronautical Society](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Aeronautical_Society) Gold Medal in 2012.[^cite_note-598] In 2015, he received an honorary doctorate in engineering and technology from [Yale University](34273.md)[^cite_note-599] and an [Institute of Electrical and Electronics Engineers Honorary Membership](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IEEE_Honorary_Membership).[^cite_note-600] Musk was elected a [Fellow of the Royal Society](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fellow_of_the_Royal_Society) (FRS) in 2018.[^cite_note-601][^cite_note-603] In 2022, Musk was elected to the [National Academy of Engineering](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Academy_of_Engineering).[^cite_note-lat20141214-604]

*[Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_\(magazine\))* has listed Musk as [one of the most influential people in the world](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_100) in 2010,[^cite_note-605] 2013,[^cite_note-606] 2018,[^cite_note-607] and 2021.[^cite_note-608] Musk was selected as *Time*'s "[Person of the Year](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_Person_of_the_Year)" for 2021. Then *Time* editor-in-chief [Edward Felsenthal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edward_Felsenthal) wrote that, "Person of the Year is a marker of influence, and few individuals have had more influence than Musk on life on Earth, and potentially life off Earth too."[^cite_note-609][^cite_note-610]

## Further reading

- [Conger, Kate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kate_Conger); [Mac, Ryan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ryan_Mac) (2024). [*Character Limit: How Elon Musk Destroyed Twitter*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Character_Limit:_How_Elon_Musk_Destroyed_Twitter) (1st hardcover ed.). New York: Penguin Press. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\)) [978-0593656136](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special\:BookSources/978-0593656136). [OCLC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/OCLC_\(identifier\)) [1432234243](https://search.worldcat.org/oclc/1432234243).

- [Farrow, Ronan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ronan_Farrow) (August 28, 2023) \[August 21, 2023 (online)]. ["Elon Musk's Shadow Rule"](https://www.newyorker.com/magazine/2023/08/28/elon-musks-shadow-rule). A Reporter at Large (story series). *[The New Yorker](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_New_Yorker)*. Retrieved August 25, 2023.

- Tarnoff, Ben (January 18, 2024). [""Ultra Hardcore" (review of Walter Isaacson, *Elon Musk*, Simon and Schuster, 2023, 670 pp.)"](https://www.nybooks.com/articles/2024/01/18/ultra-hardcore-elon-musk-walter-isaacson/). *[The New York Review of Books](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_New_York_Review_of_Books)*.&#x20;

  There is an anti-modern impulse to Musk, a craving for lordship that can't be entirely satisfied within the confines of a capitalist economy. A king doesn't have advertisers or shareholders or customers, and Musk, if he continues on his current trajectory, may very well be abandoned by all three. [Aristotle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aristotle) says a good ending should be surprising but inevitable. It's possible to imagine multiple finales for Musk that meet these criteria, but the story always begins the same way. Once upon a time in [Pretoria](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pretoria), there was a boy who wanted to be a man.

It read, "As per special request from my colleagues in the School of Engineering, I have searched Stanford's admission data base and acknowledge that you applied and were admitted to the graduate program in Material Science Engineering in 1995. Since you did not enroll, Stanford is not able to issue you an official certification document." Musk also had an explanation for the weird timing on his degrees from Penn. "I had a History and an English credit that I agreed with Penn that I would do at Stanford," he said. "The I put Stanford on deferment. Later, Penn's requirements changed so that you don't need the English and History credit. So they awarded me the degree in '97 when it was clear I was not going to go to grad school, and their requirement was no longer there."*

Retrieved March 18, 2025. It marks the first time a judge has ruled that Musk is likely exercising enough independent authority to require him to be confirmed by the Senate under the Constitution's Appointments Clause. "The record of his activities to date establishes that his role has been and will continue to be as the leader of DOGE, with the same duties and degree of continuity as if he was formally in that position,'" wrote Chuang, an appointee of former President Obama. Chuang rejected the Trump administration's argument that Musk is not the DOGE administrator and is instead merely a senior adviser to the president who has no independent authority.
